- **recon-before-decision**: Run recon BEFORE deciding at a fork — name the options, ask how the best in the field solved it, write a recon doc in researches/, reflect in two halves (why this practice is best · how it serves THIS project's goals), then decide WITH knowledge and mark the decision with an @fork block. Use when the agent faces a choice with two or more options AND a non-zero cost of being wrong, and when the human says "разведай перед решением", "как делают лучшие", "не решай сам", "/recon-before-decision". A variable name is not a fork; a decision that can hang the owner's machine is. Trigger aliases (ru):… ([MikalaiKryvusha/KAGO](https://github.com/MikalaiKryvusha/KAGO/blob/HEAD/.roo/commands/recon-before-decision.md) · ★4)

- **rectang.fr**: Comment RECTANGLE (RECTANG, alias REC) dessine un rectangle dans AutoCAD : deux coins, Cotes, Aire et Rotation, coins arrondis ou chanfreinés. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.fr.md))
- **rectang.hu**: Téglalap az AutoCAD TÉGL vagy TÉGLALAP (RECTANG) parancsával: két sarokpont, méretek, terület és forgatás, lekerekített vagy letört sarkok. Próbálja ki ingyen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.hu.md))
- **rectang.it**: Come RETTANGOLO (RECTANG, alias RT) disegna un rettangolo in AutoCAD: due angoli, Quote, Area e Rotazione, con raccordo o smusso. Provalo online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.it.md))
- **rectang.ja**: AutoCAD の RECTANG[長方形](REC)の使い方を解説します。2 つのコーナー、サイズ、面積、回転角度、フィレットで角を丸め、面取りで角を切る。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.ja.md))
- **rectang.ko**: AutoCAD RECTANG(직사각형, 캐드 렉탱, REC)으로 두 구석점, 치수, 영역(면적 지정), 회전으로 직사각형을 그리고 모깎기와 모따기로 모서리를 다듬는 방법을 설명합니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.ko.md))
- **rectang.pl**: Jak narysować prostokąt w AutoCADzie poleceniem PROSTOK (RECTANG, skrót PRO): dwa narożniki, wymiary, pole i obrót, z zaokrąglonymi lub fazowanymi narożnikami. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.pl.md))
- **rectang.pt-BR**: Como o RECTANG do AutoCAD em português, RETANGULO (alias RET), desenha um retângulo: dois cantos, Dimensões, Área e Rotação, com concordância ou chanfro. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.pt-BR.md))
- **rectang.ru**: Как нарисовать прямоугольник в AutoCAD командой ПРЯМОУГОЛЬНИК (RECTANG, ПРЯ): два угла, размеры, площадь и поворот, скругленные или со срезанными углами. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.ru.md))
- **rectang.zh-CN**: CAD 矩形怎么画：AutoCAD RECTANG（矩形，快捷键 REC）用两个角点、尺寸、面积和旋转绘制矩形，用圆角倒圆或用倒角切角。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/rectang.zh-CN.md))

- **redo.ar**: كيف يعمل أمر ⁨REDO⁩ في أوتوكاد: يعيد ما أخذه آخر ⁨U⁩ أو ⁨UNDO⁩، بعده مباشرة؛ ويعيد ⁨MREDO⁩ و⁨Ctrl+Y⁩ خطوة خطوة. جرّبه مجانًا في متصفحك. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.ar.md))
- **redo.cs**: Jak funguje příkaz ZNOVA (REDO) v AutoCADu: obnoví to, co vrátil poslední Z nebo ZPĚT, hned po něm; NZNOVA a Ctrl+Y obnovují krok po kroku. Vyzkoušejte online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.cs.md))
- **redo.de**: Wie ZLÖSCH (REDO) in AutoCAD wiederherstellt, was das letzte Z oder ZURÜCK zurückgenommen hat: nur direkt danach, MZLÖSCH und Strg+Y. Zum Testen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.de.md))
- **redo.es**: Cómo funciona REHACER (REDO) de AutoCAD en español: recupera lo que deshizo el último H o DESHACER, justo después; MREHACER y Ctrl+Y rehacen paso a paso. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.es.md))
- **redo.fr**: Comment fonctionne RETABLIR (REDO) d'AutoCAD en français : rétablir ce que U ou ANNULER vient de défaire ; MRETABLIR et Ctrl+Y, étape par étape. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.fr.md))
- **redo.hu**: Az AutoCAD ELŐRE (REDO) parancsa visszaállítja, amit az utolsó T vagy VISSZA visszavont, közvetlenül utána; a TELŐRE és a Ctrl+Y lépésenként. Ingyen, online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.hu.md))
- **redo.it**: Come funziona RIPETI (REDO) di AutoCAD in italiano: ripristina ciò che l'ultimo U o ANNULLA ha annullato; MREDO e Ctrl+Y vanno passo per passo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.it.md))
- **redo.ja**: AutoCAD の REDO[やり直し]の使い方を解説します。直前の U や UNDO で元に戻した操作をその直後に復元します。MREDO と[Ctrl]+[Y]は 1 ステップずつやり直します。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.ja.md))
- **redo.ko**: AutoCAD REDO(명령 복구, 캐드 리두)는 직전의 U나 UNDO가 되돌린 것을 바로 다시 복구합니다. MREDO와 Ctrl+Y는 한 단계씩 복구합니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.ko.md))
- **redo.pl**: Jak działa polecenie ODTWÓRZ (REDO) w AutoCADzie: przywraca to, co cofnęło ostatnie C lub COFAJ, zaraz po nim; WODTWÓRZ i Ctrl+Y krok po kroku. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.pl.md))
- **redo.pt-BR**: Como funciona o REDO do AutoCAD em português, REFAZER: devolve o que o último D ou DESFAZER desfez, logo depois; REFAZERM e Ctrl+Y refazem passo a passo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/redo.pt-BR.md))
